~/work/methane~> for T in 200K 300K 400K 450K; do
  ( cd $HOME/work/methane/$T && \
    python -u $A statepoint_mace_off24_${T}_*GPa 2>&1 | tee analysis_${T}_P.log )
done
( cd $HOME/work/methane/200K && \
  python -u $A statepoint_mace_off24_200K_rho* 2>&1 | tee analysis_200K_rho.log )
